### [MODIFICADO] `langgraph::event-streaming`

- **Hash Anterior**: `bdd5a954392d151e138a7700e068f52f66a4b2cb8da8b63bad17b870c91dbc44`
- **Hash Nuevo**: `ee4323e3362f6a8cf9620c47e2296634b29533b88b0e03ceb469936b87016e4e`

```diff
--- langgraph::event-streaming (prev: bdd5a954)
+++ langgraph::event-streaming (new: ee4323e3)
@@ -239,7 +239,11 @@
 * `content-block-finish`
 * `message-finish`
 
-Content blocks have explicit boundaries: a block starts, emits zero or more deltas, and finishes before the next block in the same message starts. This makes token streaming, reasoning blocks, tool-call blocks, and multimodal content explicit without requiring provider-specific formats. `message-finish` may include token usage; unrecoverable model-call failures arrive as message error events.
+Content blocks have explicit boundaries: a block starts, emits zero or more deltas, and finishes before the next block in the same message starts. This makes token streaming, reasoning blocks, tool-call blocks, and multimodal content explicit without requiring provider-specific formats.
+
+`message-finish` may include token usage and why the model stopped, in `finish_reason`, `normalized_finish_reason`, and `finish_state`; see [Finish reason](https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langchain/messages.md#finish-reason) for the values.
+
+Unrecoverable model-call failures arrive as message error events.
 
 Each `content-block-delta` event carries one delta for the block at its `index`:
 
```

Importing from it raises a `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`LangChainBetaWarning`}),`.`]})}),_jsx(Heading,{level:`3`,id:`features-2`,children:`Features`}),_jsxs(_components.ul,{children:[`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`One adapter, any transport`}),`: `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`MCPAdapter`}),` infers the transport from its target — a URL, a local script over stdio, an in-process server, an `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`MCPConfig`}),` dict for `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/langchain/mcp/connections#multiple-servers`,children:`multiple servers`}),`, or a pre-built `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`fastmcp.Client`}),`. `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`await adapter.list_tools()`}),` returns LangChain tools ready for `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`create_agent`}),`. See the `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/langchain/mcp`,children:`MCP overview`}),`.`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`Interrupt-driven elicitation`}),`: when a server asks for input mid-call, `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`MCPAdapter`}),` surfaces the question as a LangGraph `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`interrupt()`}),`, so a human answers and the run resumes. See `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/langchain/mcp/tools#elicitation`,children:`Elicitation`}),`.`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`Authentication through FastMCP`}),`: bearer tokens, full OAuth 2.1 with dynamic client registration, or any `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`httpx.Auth`}),`, including `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/langchain/mcp/auth#per-user-authentication`,children:`per-user authentication`}),` in a deployment. See `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/langchain/mcp/auth`,children:`Authentication`}),`.`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`Richer tool metadata`}),`: each tool carries its MCP provenance under an `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`mcp`}),` namespace on its metadata, including annotations such as `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`destructive_hint`}),` for `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/langchain/mcp/tools#human-in-the-loop`,children:`gating destructive tools behind approval`}),`. See `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/langchain/mcp/tools#tool-metadata`,children:`Tool metadata`}),`.`]}),`\n`]}),_jsx(Heading,{level:`3`,id:`migrating`,children:`Migrating`}),_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[`Moving from `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`langchain-mcp-adapters`}),`? `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`MultiServerMCPClient`}),` is replaced by `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`MCPAdapter`}),`, and several features have changed or been removed. See the `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/migrate/langchain-mcp-adapters`,children:`migration guide`}),`.`]})]}),`\n`,_jsxs(Update,{label:`Jul 24, 2026`,tags:[`deepagents`],rss:{title:`Jul 24, 2026 - deepagents`},id:`jul-24%2C-2026`,children:[_jsxs(Heading,{level:`2`,id:`deepagents-v0-7-0`,children:[_jsx(_components.code,{children:`deepagents`}),` v0.7.0`]}),_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[`A leaner, more configurable harness by default. On a default-agent turn, input tokens drop `,_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`65%`}),` (5,395 → 1,895), validated against our `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://www.langchain.com/blog/how-we-benchmark-deep-agents`,children:`revamped evaluation suite`}),` with no quality regression.`]}),_jsx(Heading,{level:`3`,id:`optimizations`,children:`Optimizations`}),_jsxs(_components.ul,{children:[`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`Lean prompts by default`}),`: The authored base prompt starts empty and tool-usage prose that duplicated tool schemas has been trimmed. Isolated to the default agent’s tool schemas, total description tokens drop `,_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`43%`}),` (4,005 → 2,302); combined with the empty base prompt and opt-in todos, a default-agent turn’s input tokens drop `,_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`65%`}),` (5,395 → 1,895). Tool behavior is unchanged. (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4859`,children:`#4859`}),`, `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4979`,children:`#4979`}),`, `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/5009`,children:`#5009`}),`)`]}),`\n`]}),_jsx(Heading,{level:`3`,id:`features-3`,children:`Features`}),_jsxs(_components.ul,{children:[`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/deepagents/customization#middleware`,children:`Override a default middleware instance`})}),`: A `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`middleware=`}),` (or subagent `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`middleware`}),`) instance whose `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`.name`}),` matches a built-in now replaces that default in place, rather than erroring on a duplicate. For example, pass your own `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`SummarizationMiddleware(...)`}),` to change the token trigger or summary model without disabling the built-in default. (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4251`,children:`#4251`}),`)`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`Filesystem tools`}),`: New `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/deepagents/tools#built-in-harness-tools`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`delete`})}),` tool removes a file or recursively removes a directory (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/3659`,children:`#3659`}),`, `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/3851`,children:`#3851`}),`); `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`write_file`}),` now overwrites an existing file instead of erroring (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4109`,children:`#4109`}),`); `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`FilesystemMiddleware`}),` accepts a `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/deepagents/overview#virtual-filesystem-access`,children:`tool allowlist`}),` to expose only selected built-in tools (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4325`,children:`#4325`}),`, `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4698`,children:`#4698`}),`); and reads and searches are tuned for open models — paginated `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`read_file`}),` reports total and remaining lines plus the next `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`offset`}),` (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4540`,children:`#4540`}),`), `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`grep`}),`/`,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`glob`}),` return partial results with a `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`truncated`}),` flag instead of hanging on large trees (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4063`,children:`#4063`}),`), and `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`grep`}),` gains a 1,000-match cap with streamed output and optional context lines (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4570`,children:`#4570`}),`, `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4706`,children:`#4706`}),`).`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`More prompt-caching support`}),`: Bedrock prompt caching via the `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`deepagents[aws]`}),` extra (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/issues/4108`,children:`#4108`}),`), and automatic Fireworks prompt-cache session affinity (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4598`,children:`#4598`}),`).`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`NVIDIA support`}),`: A built-in Nemotron 3 Ultra harness profile plus NIM app-origin attribution. (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4192`,children:`#4192`}),`, `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4455`,children:`#4455`}),`)`]}),`\n`]}),_jsx(Heading,{level:`3`,id:`breaking-changes-2`,children:`Breaking changes`}),_jsxs(_components.ul,{children:[`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`Planning todos are opt-in`}),`: `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`create_deep_agent`}),` no longer includes `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`TodoListMiddleware`}),` by default, so the `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`write_todos`}),` tool, `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`todos`}),` state channel, and todo-planning prompt are absent unless restored with `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`middleware=[TodoListMiddleware()]`}),`. (The OpenAI Codex harness profile still opts in automatically.) (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4929`,children:`#4929`}),`)`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`Backend compatibility shims removed`}),`: Pass concrete `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`BackendProtocol`}),` instances instead of factories, configure `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`StoreBackend`}),` with an explicit `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`namespace`}),`, and use the current `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`ls`}),` / `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`glob`}),` / `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`grep`}),` / `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`ReadResult`}),` APIs. Removed symbols include `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`BackendFactory`}),`, `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`BACKEND_TYPES`}),`, `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`FileFormat`}),`, and `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`Unset`}),`. New files store string `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`FileData.content`}),`; older `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`list[str]`}),` content stays readable and converts on next write. (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4541`,children:`#4541`}),`)`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`Output format changes`}),`: Empty `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`ls`}),` / `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`glob`}),` output is now `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`No files found`}),` instead of `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`[]`}),`, and `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`read_file`}),` no longer renders a fixed-width `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`cat -n`}),`-style gutter — update any parsers of raw tool output. (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4561`,children:`#4561`}),`)`]}),`\n`]}),_jsx(_components.p,{children:`Copy the following prompt into your AI coding assistant to migrate a codebase for these breaking changes:`}),_jsx(Prompt,{description:`Migrate a deepagents codebase from v0.6.x to v0.7.`,icon:`arrow-right`,actions:[`copy`],children:'Migrate this codebase from `deepagents` v0.6.x to v0.7 to account for the following breaking changes:\\n\\n1. `create_deep_agent` no longer includes `TodoListMiddleware` by default. If this codebase relies on the `write_todos` tool, the `todos` state channel, or the todo-planning prompt, restore it by importing `TodoListMiddleware` from `langchain.agents.middleware` (not `deepagents`) and passing it to `create_deep_agent`:\\n\\n   ```python  theme={\"theme\":{\"light\":\"catppuccin-latte\",\"dark\":\"catppuccin-mocha\"}}\\n   from langchain.agents.middleware import TodoListMiddleware\\n   from deepagents import create_deep_agent\\n\\n   agent = create_deep_agent(middleware=[TodoListMiddleware()])\\n   ```\\n\\n2. Backend compatibility shims were removed: `BackendFactory`, `BACKEND_TYPES`, `FileFormat`, and `Unset` no longer exist. Replace any backend factories with concrete `BackendProtocol` instances, and add an explicit `namespace` to every `StoreBackend` configuration:\\n\\n   ```python  theme={\"theme\":{\"light\":\"catppuccin-latte\",\"dark\":\"catppuccin-mocha\"}}\\n   from deepagents import create_deep_agent\\n   from deepagents.backends import StoreBackend\\n\\n   # Before (v0.6.x): factory callable, and StoreBackend with no explicit namespace\\n   agent = create_deep_agent(backend=lambda rt: StoreBackend())  # [!code --]\\n\\n   # After (v0.7): concrete backend instance with an explicit namespace\\n   agent = create_deep_agent(backend=StoreBackend(namespace=lambda rt: (rt.server_info.user.identity,)))  # [!code ++]\\n   ```\\n\\n   Also update calls to use the current `ls`, `glob`, `grep`, and `ReadResult` APIs.\\n\\n3. Tool output formats changed: empty `ls` / `glob` output is now the string `No files found` instead of `[]`, and `read_file` no longer renders a fixed-width `cat -n`-style line-number gutter. Update any code that parses these tool outputs.\\n\\nSearch the codebase for usages of the removed symbols and for parsing logic that depends on the old output formats, apply the necessary changes, and flag anything that needs manual review.\\n'})]}),`\n`,_jsxs(Update,{label:`May 12, 2026`,tags:[`deepagents`],rss:{title:`May 12, 2026 - langchain`},id:`may-12%2C-2026`,children:[_jsxs(Heading,{level:`2`,id:`deepagents-v0-6-0`,children:[_jsx(_components.code,{children:`deepagents`}),` v0.6.0`]}),_jsxs(_components.ul,{children:[`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/deepagents/interpreters`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`CodeInterpreterMiddleware`})})}),`: (experimental) `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`deepagents`}),` now supports code execution and programmatic tool calling through a scoped QuickJS runtime.`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[`Supports `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`version=\"v3\"`}),` in `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`stream_events`}),` / `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`astream_events`}),`. Refer to the `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/deepagents/event-streaming`,children:`event streaming`}),` guide for details.`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsxs(_components.strong,{children:[_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/langgraph/pregel#deltachannel`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`DeltaChannel`})}),` (beta)`]}),` (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://www.langchain.com/blog/delta-channels-evolving-agent-runtime`,children:`blog`}),`): Deep Agents now uses `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`DeltaChannel`}),` for message history and agent files. Rather than re-serializing the full accumulated value into every checkpoint, only the incremental delta written at each step is stored — keeping checkpoint sizes small as threads grow long.`]}),`\n`]}),_jsx(Warning,{children:_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`Rolling back from v0.6.0 is not supported once threads have persisted.`}),` Deep Agents v0.6.0 changes persisted message history and agent files to `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`DeltaChannel`}),`, which writes checkpoints in a new format that earlier versions cannot read. Downgrading to an earlier Deep Agents version switches these channels back to non-delta channels, leaving existing delta checkpoints unreadable and causing incomplete or incorrect state reconstruction. If you need to roll back, use the `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/langgraph/tree/main/examples/delta-channel-dump`,children:`delta-channel-dump recovery script`}),` to migrate affected threads, or discard them, before downgrading. More generally, avoid switching a persisted channel between delta and non-delta representations. See `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/langgraph/pregel#version-compatibility-and-rollbacks`,children:`Version compatibility and channel changes`}),`.`]})}),_jsxs(_components.ul,{children:[`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/deepagents/profiles`,children:`Harness profiles`})}),`: Register per-provider or per-model configuration bundles (`,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`HarnessProfile`}),`) that `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`create_deep_agent`}),` applies automatically when a model is selected — system-prompt tweaks, tool overrides, middleware changes, and subagent defaults — without modifying the call site.`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/deepagents/backends#contexthubbackend`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`ContextHubBackend`})})}),` (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://www.langchain.com/blog/introducing-context-hub`,children:`blog`}),`): A new filesystem backend backed by LangSmith Hub. Agent files — skills, memories, and other persisted context — are stored as Hub commits, giving you version history on every write and LangSmith-native durability without provisioning a separate LangGraph store.`]}),`\n`]})]}),`\n`,_jsxs(Update,{label:`May 12, 2026`,tags:[`langchain`],rss:{title:`May 12, 2026 - langchain`},id:`may-12%2C-2026-2`,children:[_jsxs(Heading,{level:`2`,id:`langchain-v1-3-0`,children:[_jsx(_components.code,{children:`langchain`}),` v1.3.0`]}),_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[`This release adds support for `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`version=\"v3\"`}),` in `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`stream_events`}),` / `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`astream_events`}),` for `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`langchain`}),` agents. Refer to the `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/langchain/event-streaming`,children:`event streaming`}),` guide for details.`]})]}),`\n`,_jsxs(Update,{label:`May 12, 2026`,tags:[`langgraph`],rss:{title:`May 12, 2026 - langgraph`},id:`may-12%2C-2026-3`,children:[_jsxs(Heading,{level:`2`,id:`langgraph-v1-2-0`,children:[_jsx(_components.code,{children:`langgraph`}),` v1.2.0`]}),_jsx(_components.p,{children:`This release adds finer-grained control over node execution (timeouts, error recovery, and graceful shutdown), a new channel type that cuts checkpoint overhead for long-running threads, and a new content-block-centric streaming API (v3) with typed, per-channel projections.`}),_jsxs(_components.ul,{children:[`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[`\n`,_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[_jsxs(_components.strong,{children:[_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/langgraph/pregel#deltachannel`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`DeltaChannel`})}),` (beta)`]}),`: A new channel type that stores only the incremental delta at each step rather than re-serializing the full accumulated value. Most useful for channels that grow large over time, for example a message list in a long-running thread. Use `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`snapshot_frequency=K`}),` to write a full snapshot every K steps and bound read latency.`]}),`\n`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[`\n`,_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/langgraph/fault-tolerance#timeouts`,children:`Per-node timeouts`})}),`: Pass `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`timeout=`}),` to `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://reference.langchain.com/python/langgraph/graph/state/StateGraph/add_node`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`add_node`})}),` to cap how long a single attempt may run. Set a hard wall-clock limit (`,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`run_timeout`}),`), an idle limit that resets on progress (`,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`idle_timeout`}),`), or both via `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://reference.langchain.com/python/langgraph/types/TimeoutPolicy`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`TimeoutPolicy`})}),`. When the limit fires, LangGraph raises `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://reference.langchain.com/python/langgraph/errors/NodeTimeoutError`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`NodeTimeoutError`})}),`, clears writes from that attempt, and hands off to the retry policy. Async nodes only.`]}),`\n`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[`\n`,_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/langgraph/fault-tolerance#error-handling`,children:`Node-level error handlers`})}),`: Pass `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`error_handler=`}),` to `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://reference.langchain.com/python/langgraph/graph/state/StateGraph/add_node`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`add_node`})}),` to run a recovery function after all retries are exhausted. See the `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/migrate/langchain-mcp-adapters`,children:`migration guide`}),`.`]})]}),`\n`,_jsxs(Update,{label:`Jul 24, 2026`,tags:[`deepagents`],rss:{title:`Jul 24, 2026 - deepagents`},id:`jul-24%2C-2026`,children:[_jsxs(Heading,{level:`2`,id:`deepagents-v0-7-0`,children:[_jsx(_components.code,{children:`deepagents`}),` v0.7.0`]}),_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[`A leaner, more configurable harness by default. On a default-agent turn, input tokens drop `,_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`65%`}),` (5,395 → 1,895), validated against our `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://www.langchain.com/blog/how-we-benchmark-deep-agents`,children:`revamped evaluation suite`}),` with no quality regression.`]}),_jsx(Heading,{level:`3`,id:`optimizations`,children:`Optimizations`}),_jsxs(_components.ul,{children:[`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`Lean prompts by default`}),`: The authored base prompt starts empty and tool-usage prose that duplicated tool schemas has been trimmed. Isolated to the default agent’s tool schemas, total description tokens drop `,_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`43%`}),` (4,005 → 2,302); combined with the empty base prompt and opt-in todos, a default-agent turn’s input tokens drop `,_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`65%`}),` (5,395 → 1,895). Tool behavior is unchanged. (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4859`,children:`#4859`}),`, `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4979`,children:`#4979`}),`, `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/5009`,children:`#5009`}),`)`]}),`\n`]}),_jsx(Heading,{level:`3`,id:`features-3`,children:`Features`}),_jsxs(_components.ul,{children:[`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/deepagents/customization#middleware`,children:`Override a default middleware instance`})}),`: A `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`middleware=`}),` (or subagent `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`middleware`}),`) instance whose `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`.name`}),` matches a built-in now replaces that default in place, rather than erroring on a duplicate. For example, pass your own `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`SummarizationMiddleware(...)`}),` to change the token trigger or summary model without disabling the built-in default. (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4251`,children:`#4251`}),`)`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`Filesystem tools`}),`: New `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/deepagents/tools#built-in-harness-tools`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`delete`})}),` tool removes a file or recursively removes a directory (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/3659`,children:`#3659`}),`, `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/3851`,children:`#3851`}),`); `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`write_file`}),` now overwrites an existing file instead of erroring (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4109`,children:`#4109`}),`); `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`FilesystemMiddleware`}),` accepts a `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/deepagents/overview#virtual-filesystem-access`,children:`tool allowlist`}),` to expose only selected built-in tools (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4325`,children:`#4325`}),`, `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4698`,children:`#4698`}),`); and reads and searches are tuned for open models — paginated `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`read_file`}),` reports total and remaining lines plus the next `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`offset`}),` (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4540`,children:`#4540`}),`), `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`grep`}),`/`,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`glob`}),` return partial results with a `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`truncated`}),` flag instead of hanging on large trees (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4063`,children:`#4063`}),`), and `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`grep`}),` gains a 1,000-match cap with streamed output and optional context lines (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4570`,children:`#4570`}),`, `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4706`,children:`#4706`}),`).`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`More prompt-caching support`}),`: Bedrock prompt caching via the `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`deepagents[aws]`}),` extra (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/issues/4108`,children:`#4108`}),`), and automatic Fireworks prompt-cache session affinity (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4598`,children:`#4598`}),`).`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`NVIDIA support`}),`: A built-in Nemotron 3 Ultra harness profile plus NIM app-origin attribution. (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4192`,children:`#4192`}),`, `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4455`,children:`#4455`}),`)`]}),`\n`]}),_jsx(Heading,{level:`3`,id:`breaking-changes-2`,children:`Breaking changes`}),_jsxs(_components.ul,{children:[`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`Planning todos are opt-in`}),`: `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`create_deep_agent`}),` no longer includes `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`TodoListMiddleware`}),` by default, so the `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`write_todos`}),` tool, `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`todos`}),` state channel, and todo-planning prompt are absent unless restored with `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`middleware=[TodoListMiddleware()]`}),`. (The OpenAI Codex harness profile still opts in automatically.) (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4929`,children:`#4929`}),`)`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`Backend compatibility shims removed`}),`: Pass concrete `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`BackendProtocol`}),` instances instead of factories, configure `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`StoreBackend`}),` with an explicit `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`namespace`}),`, and use the current `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`ls`}),` / `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`glob`}),` / `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`grep`}),` / `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`ReadResult`}),` APIs. Removed symbols include `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`BackendFactory`}),`, `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`BACKEND_TYPES`}),`, `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`FileFormat`}),`, and `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`Unset`}),`. New files store string `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`FileData.content`}),`; older `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`list[str]`}),` content stays readable and converts on next write. (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4541`,children:`#4541`}),`)`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`Output format changes`}),`: Empty `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`ls`}),` / `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`glob`}),` output is now `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`No files found`}),` instead of `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`[]`}),`, and `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`read_file`}),` no longer renders a fixed-width `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`cat -n`}),`-style gutter — update any parsers of raw tool output. (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4561`,children:`#4561`}),`)`]}),`\n`]}),_jsx(_components.p,{children:`Copy the following prompt into your AI coding assistant to migrate a codebase for these breaking changes:`}),_jsx(Prompt,{description:`Migrate a deepagents codebase from v0.6.x to v0.7.`,icon:`arrow-right`,actions:[`copy`],children:'Migrate this codebase from `deepagents` v0.6.x to v0.7 to account for the following breaking changes:\\n\\n1. `create_deep_agent` no longer includes `TodoListMiddleware` by default. If this codebase relies on the `write_todos` tool, the `todos` state channel, or the todo-planning prompt, restore it by importing `TodoListMiddleware` from `langchain.agents.middleware` (not `deepagents`) and passing it to `create_deep_agent`:\\n\\n   ```python  theme={\"theme\":{\"light\":\"catppuccin-latte\",\"dark\":\"catppuccin-mocha\"}}\\n   from langchain.agents.middleware import TodoListMiddleware\\n   from deepagents import create_deep_agent\\n\\n   agent = create_deep_agent(middleware=[TodoListMiddleware()])\\n   ```\\n\\n2. Backend compatibility shims were removed: `BackendFactory`, `BACKEND_TYPES`, `FileFormat`, and `Unset` no longer exist. Replace any backend factories with concrete `BackendProtocol` instances, and add an explicit `namespace` to every `StoreBackend` configuration:\\n\\n   ```python  theme={\"theme\":{\"light\":\"catppuccin-latte\",\"dark\":\"catppuccin-mocha\"}}\\n   from deepagents import create_deep_agent\\n   from deepagents.backends import StoreBackend\\n\\n   # Before (v0.6.x): factory callable, and StoreBackend with no explicit namespace\\n   agent = create_deep_agent(backend=lambda rt: StoreBackend())  # [!code --]\\n\\n   # After (v0.7): concrete backend instance with an explicit namespace\\n   agent = create_deep_agent(backend=StoreBackend(namespace=lambda rt: (rt.server_info.user.identity,)))  # [!code ++]\\n   ```\\n\\n   Also update calls to use the current `ls`, `glob`, `grep`, and `ReadResult` APIs.\\n\\n3. Tool output formats changed: empty `ls` / `glob` output is now the string `No files found` instead of `[]`, and `read_file` no longer renders a fixed-width `cat -n`-style line-number gutter. Update any code that parses these tool outputs.\\n\\nSearch the codebase for usages of the removed symbols and for parsing logic that depends on the old output formats, apply the necessary changes, and flag anything that needs manual review.\\n'})]}),`\n`,_jsxs(Update,{label:`May 12, 2026`,tags:[`deepagents`],rss:{title:`May 12, 2026 - langchain`},id:`may-12%2C-2026`,children:[_jsxs(Heading,{level:`2`,id:`deepagents-v0-6-0`,children:[_jsx(_components.code,{children:`deepagents`}),` v0.6.0`]}),_jsxs(_components.ul,{children:[`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/deepagents/interpreters`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`CodeInterpreterMiddleware`})})}),`: (experimental) `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`deepagents`}),` now supports code execution and programmatic tool calling through a scoped QuickJS runtime.`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[`Supports `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`version=\"v3\"`}),` in `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`stream_events`}),` / `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`astream_events`}),`. Refer to the `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/deepagents/event-streaming`,children:`event streaming`}),` guide for details.`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsxs(_components.strong,{children:[_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/langgraph/pregel#deltachannel`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`DeltaChannel`})}),` (beta)`]}),` (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://www.langchain.com/blog/delta-channels-evolving-agent-runtime`,children:`blog`}),`): Deep Agents now uses `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`DeltaChannel`}),` for message history and agent files. Rather than re-serializing the full accumulated value into every checkpoint, only the incremental delta written at each step is stored — keeping checkpoint sizes small as threads grow long.`]}),`\n`]}),_jsx(Warning,{children:_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`Rolling back from v0.6.0 is not supported once threads have persisted.`}),` Deep Agents v0.6.0 changes persisted message history and agent files to `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`DeltaChannel`}),`, which writes checkpoints in a new format that earlier versions cannot read. Downgrading to an earlier Deep Agents version switches these channels back to non-delta channels, leaving existing delta checkpoints unreadable and causing incomplete or incorrect state reconstruction. If you need to roll back, use the `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/langgraph/tree/main/examples/delta-channel-dump`,children:`delta-channel-dump recovery script`}),` to migrate affected threads, or discard them, before downgrading. More generally, avoid switching a persisted channel between delta and non-delta representations. See `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/langgraph/pregel#version-compatibility-and-rollbacks`,children:`Version compatibility and channel changes`}),`.`]})}),_jsxs(_components.ul,{children:[`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/deepagents/profiles`,children:`Harness profiles`})}),`: Register per-provider or per-model configuration bundles (`,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`HarnessProfile`}),`) that `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`create_deep_agent`}),` applies automatically when a model is selected — system-prompt tweaks, tool overrides, middleware changes, and subagent defaults — without modifying the call site.`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/deepagents/backends#contexthubbackend`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`ContextHubBackend`})})}),` (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://www.langchain.com/blog/introducing-context-hub`,children:`blog`}),`): A new filesystem backend backed by LangSmith Hub. Agent files — skills, memories, and other persisted context — are stored as Hub commits, giving you version history on every write and LangSmith-native durability without provisioning a separate LangGraph store.`]}),`\n`]})]}),`\n`,_jsxs(Update,{label:`May 12, 2026`,tags:[`langchain`],rss:{title:`May 12, 2026 - langchain`},id:`may-12%2C-2026-2`,children:[_jsxs(Heading,{level:`2`,id:`langchain-v1-3-0`,children:[_jsx(_components.code,{children:`langchain`}),` v1.3.0`]}),_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[`This release adds support for `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`version=\"v3\"`}),` in `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`stream_events`}),` / `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`astream_events`}),` for `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`langchain`}),` agents. Most useful for channels that grow large over time, for example a message list in a long-running thread. Use `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`snapshot_frequency=K`}),` to write a full snapshot every K steps and bound read latency.`]}),`\n`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[`\n`,_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/langgraph/fault-tolerance#timeouts`,children:`Per-node timeouts`})}),`: Pass `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`timeout=`}),` to `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://reference.langchain.com/python/langgraph/graph/state/StateGraph/add_node`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`add_node`})}),` to cap how long a single attempt may run. Set a hard wall-clock limit (`,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`run_timeout`}),`), an idle limit that resets on progress (`,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`idle_timeout`}),`), or both via `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://reference.langchain.com/python/langgraph/types/TimeoutPolicy`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`TimeoutPolicy`})}),`. When the limit fires, LangGraph raises `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://reference.langchain.com/python/langgraph/errors/NodeTimeoutError`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`NodeTimeoutError`})}),`, clears writes from that attempt, and hands off to the retry policy. Async nodes only.`]}),`\n`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[`\n`,_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/langgraph/fault-tolerance#error-handling`,children:`Node-level error handlers`})}),`: Pass `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`error_handler=`}),` to `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://reference.langchain.com/python/langgraph/graph/state/StateGraph/add_node`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`add_node`})}),` to run a recovery function after all retries are exhausted. The handler receives a typed `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://reference.langchain.com/python/langgraph/errors/NodeError`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`NodeError`})}),` and can return a `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://reference.langchain.com/python/langgraph/types/Command`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`Command`})}),` to update state and route to a different node, useful for Saga/compensation patterns.`]}),`\n`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[`\n`,_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/langgraph/fault-tolerance#graceful-shutdown`,children:`Graceful shutdown`})}),`: Stop an in-flight run cooperatively after the current superstep completes, and save a resumable checkpoint. the run raises `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`GraphDrained`}),` and can be resumed later with the same config.`]}),`\n`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[`\n`,_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`New event streaming API (beta)`}),`: Pass `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`version=\"v3\"`}),` to `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`stream_events()`}),` / `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`astream_events()`}),` for a content-block-centric protocol with typed, per-channel projections (`,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`run.values`}),`, `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`run.messages`}),`, `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`run.lifecycle`}),`, `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`run.subgraphs`}),`) plus opt-in transformers for updates, custom events, checkpoints, tasks, and debug. `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`run.messages`}),` yields one `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`ChatModelStream`}),` per LLM call with typed sub-projections for text, reasoning, tool calls, and usage. `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`version=\"v1\"`}),` and `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`version=\"v2\"`}),` are unchanged.`]}),`\n`]}),`\n`]}),_jsx(_components.p,{children:`Timeouts and error handlers are Python-only; See the `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/migrate/langchain-mcp-adapters`,children:`migration guide`}),`.`]})]}),`\n`,_jsxs(Update,{label:`Jul 24, 2026`,tags:[`deepagents`],rss:{title:`Jul 24, 2026 - deepagents`},id:`jul-24%2C-2026`,children:[_jsxs(Heading,{level:`2`,id:`deepagents-v0-7-0`,children:[_jsx(_components.code,{children:`deepagents`}),` v0.7.0`]}),_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[`A leaner, more configurable harness by default. On a default-agent turn, input tokens drop `,_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`65%`}),` (5,395 → 1,895), validated against our `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://www.langchain.com/blog/how-we-benchmark-deep-agents`,children:`revamped evaluation suite`}),` with no quality regression.`]}),_jsx(Heading,{level:`3`,id:`optimizations`,children:`Optimizations`}),_jsxs(_components.ul,{children:[`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`Lean prompts by default`}),`: The authored base prompt starts empty and tool-usage prose that duplicated tool schemas has been trimmed. Isolated to the default agent’s tool schemas, total description tokens drop `,_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`43%`}),` (4,005 → 2,302); combined with the empty base prompt and opt-in todos, a default-agent turn’s input tokens drop `,_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`65%`}),` (5,395 → 1,895). Tool behavior is unchanged. (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4859`,children:`#4859`}),`, `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4979`,children:`#4979`}),`, `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/5009`,children:`#5009`}),`)`]}),`\n`]}),_jsx(Heading,{level:`3`,id:`features-3`,children:`Features`}),_jsxs(_components.ul,{children:[`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/deepagents/customization#middleware`,children:`Override a default middleware instance`})}),`: A `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`middleware=`}),` (or subagent `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`middleware`}),`) instance whose `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`.name`}),` matches a built-in now replaces that default in place, rather than erroring on a duplicate. For example, pass your own `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`SummarizationMiddleware(...)`}),` to change the token trigger or summary model without disabling the built-in default. (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4251`,children:`#4251`}),`)`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`Filesystem tools`}),`: New `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/deepagents/tools#built-in-harness-tools`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`delete`})}),` tool removes a file or recursively removes a directory (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/3659`,children:`#3659`}),`, `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/3851`,children:`#3851`}),`); `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`write_file`}),` now overwrites an existing file instead of erroring (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4109`,children:`#4109`}),`); `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`FilesystemMiddleware`}),` accepts a `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/deepagents/overview#virtual-filesystem-access`,children:`tool allowlist`}),` to expose only selected built-in tools (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4325`,children:`#4325`}),`, `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4698`,children:`#4698`}),`); and reads and searches are tuned for open models — paginated `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`read_file`}),` reports total and remaining lines plus the next `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`offset`}),` (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4540`,children:`#4540`}),`), `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`grep`}),`/`,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`glob`}),` return partial results with a `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`truncated`}),` flag instead of hanging on large trees (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4063`,children:`#4063`}),`), and `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`grep`}),` gains a 1,000-match cap with streamed output and optional context lines (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4570`,children:`#4570`}),`, `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4706`,children:`#4706`}),`).`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`More prompt-caching support`}),`: Bedrock prompt caching via the `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`deepagents[aws]`}),` extra (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/issues/4108`,children:`#4108`}),`), and automatic Fireworks prompt-cache session affinity (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4598`,children:`#4598`}),`).`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`NVIDIA support`}),`: A built-in Nemotron 3 Ultra harness profile plus NIM app-origin attribution. (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4192`,children:`#4192`}),`, `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4455`,children:`#4455`}),`)`]}),`\n`]}),_jsx(Heading,{level:`3`,id:`breaking-changes-2`,children:`Breaking changes`}),_jsxs(_components.ul,{children:[`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`Planning todos are opt-in`}),`: `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`create_deep_agent`}),` no longer includes `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`TodoListMiddleware`}),` by default, so the `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`write_todos`}),` tool, `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`todos`}),` state channel, and todo-planning prompt are absent unless restored with `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`middleware=[TodoListMiddleware()]`}),`. (The OpenAI Codex harness profile still opts in automatically.) (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4929`,children:`#4929`}),`)`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`Backend compatibility shims removed`}),`: Pass concrete `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`BackendProtocol`}),` instances instead of factories, configure `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`StoreBackend`}),` with an explicit `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`namespace`}),`, and use the current `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`ls`}),` / `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`glob`}),` / `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`grep`}),` / `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`ReadResult`}),` APIs. Removed symbols include `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`BackendFactory`}),`, `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`BACKEND_TYPES`}),`, `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`FileFormat`}),`, and `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`Unset`}),`. New files store string `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`FileData.content`}),`; older `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`list[str]`}),` content stays readable and converts on next write. (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4541`,children:`#4541`}),`)`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`Output format changes`}),`: Empty `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`ls`}),` / `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`glob`}),` output is now `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`No files found`}),` instead of `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`[]`}),`, and `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`read_file`}),` no longer renders a fixed-width `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`cat -n`}),`-style gutter — update any parsers of raw tool output. (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4561`,children:`#4561`}),`)`]}),`\n`]}),_jsx(_components.p,{children:`Copy the following prompt into your AI coding assistant to migrate a codebase for these breaking changes:`}),_jsx(Prompt,{description:`Migrate a deepagents codebase from v0.6.x to v0.7.`,icon:`arrow-right`,actions:[`copy`],children:'Migrate this codebase from `deepagents` v0.6.x to v0.7 to account for the following breaking changes:\\n\\n1. `create_deep_agent` no longer includes `TodoListMiddleware` by default. If this codebase relies on the `write_todos` tool, the `todos` state channel, or the todo-planning prompt, restore it by importing `TodoListMiddleware` from `langchain.agents.middleware` (not `deepagents`) and passing it to `create_deep_agent`:\\n\\n   ```python  theme={\"theme\":{\"light\":\"catppuccin-latte\",\"dark\":\"catppuccin-mocha\"}}\\n   from langchain.agents.middleware import TodoListMiddleware\\n   from deepagents import create_deep_agent\\n\\n   agent = create_deep_agent(middleware=[TodoListMiddleware()])\\n   ```\\n\\n2. Backend compatibility shims were removed: `BackendFactory`, `BACKEND_TYPES`, `FileFormat`, and `Unset` no longer exist. Replace any backend factories with concrete `BackendProtocol` instances, and add an explicit `namespace` to every `StoreBackend` configuration:\\n\\n   ```python  theme={\"theme\":{\"light\":\"catppuccin-latte\",\"dark\":\"catppuccin-mocha\"}}\\n   from deepagents import create_deep_agent\\n   from deepagents.backends import StoreBackend\\n\\n   # Before (v0.6.x): factory callable, and StoreBackend with no explicit namespace\\n   agent = create_deep_agent(backend=lambda rt: StoreBackend())  # [!code --]\\n\\n   # After (v0.7): concrete backend instance with an explicit namespace\\n   agent = create_deep_agent(backend=StoreBackend(namespace=lambda rt: (rt.server_info.user.identity,)))  # [!code ++]\\n   ```\\n\\n   Also update calls to use the current `ls`, `glob`, `grep`, and `ReadResult` APIs.\\n\\n3. Tool output formats changed: empty `ls` / `glob` output is now the string `No files found` instead of `[]`, and `read_file` no longer renders a fixed-width `cat -n`-style line-number gutter. Update any code that parses these tool outputs.\\n\\nSearch the codebase for usages of the removed symbols and for parsing logic that depends on the old output formats, apply the necessary changes, and flag anything that needs manual review.\\n'})]}),`\n`,_jsxs(Update,{label:`May 12, 2026`,tags:[`deepagents`],rss:{title:`May 12, 2026 - langchain`},id:`may-12%2C-2026`,children:[_jsxs(Heading,{level:`2`,id:`deepagents-v0-6-0`,children:[_jsx(_components.code,{children:`deepagents`}),` v0.6.0`]}),_jsxs(_components.ul,{children:[`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/deepagents/interpreters`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`CodeInterpreterMiddleware`})})}),`: (experimental) `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`deepagents`}),` now supports code execution and programmatic tool calling through a scoped QuickJS runtime.`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[`Supports `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`version=\"v3\"`}),` in `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`stream_events`}),` / `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`astream_events`}),`. Refer to the `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/deepagents/event-streaming`,children:`event streaming`}),` guide for details.`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsxs(_components.strong,{children:[_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/langgraph/pregel#deltachannel`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`DeltaChannel`})}),` (beta)`]}),` (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://www.langchain.com/blog/delta-channels-evolving-agent-runtime`,children:`blog`}),`): Deep Agents now uses `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`DeltaChannel`}),` for message history and agent files. Rather than re-serializing the full accumulated value into every checkpoint, only the incremental delta written at each step is stored — keeping checkpoint sizes small as threads grow long.`]}),`\n`]}),_jsx(Warning,{children:_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`Rolling back from v0.6.0 is not supported once threads have persisted.`}),` Deep Agents v0.6.0 changes persisted message history and agent files to `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`DeltaChannel`}),`, which writes checkpoints in a new format that earlier versions cannot read. Downgrading to an earlier Deep Agents version switches these channels back to non-delta channels, leaving existing delta checkpoints unreadable and causing incomplete or incorrect state reconstruction. If you need to roll back, use the `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/langgraph/tree/main/examples/delta-channel-dump`,children:`delta-channel-dump recovery script`}),` to migrate affected threads, or discard them, before downgrading. More generally, avoid switching a persisted channel between delta and non-delta representations. See `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/langgraph/pregel#version-compatibility-and-rollbacks`,children:`Version compatibility and channel changes`}),`.`]})}),_jsxs(_components.ul,{children:[`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/deepagents/profiles`,children:`Harness profiles`})}),`: Register per-provider or per-model configuration bundles (`,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`HarnessProfile`}),`) that `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`create_deep_agent`}),` applies automatically when a model is selected — system-prompt tweaks, tool overrides, middleware changes, and subagent defaults — without modifying the call site.`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/deepagents/backends#contexthubbackend`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`ContextHubBackend`})})}),` (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://www.langchain.com/blog/introducing-context-hub`,children:`blog`}),`): A new filesystem backend backed by LangSmith Hub. Agent files — skills, memories, and other persisted context — are stored as Hub commits, giving you version history on every write and LangSmith-native durability without provisioning a separate LangGraph store.`]}),`\n`]})]}),`\n`,_jsxs(Update,{label:`May 12, 2026`,tags:[`langchain`],rss:{title:`May 12, 2026 - langchain`},id:`may-12%2C-2026-2`,children:[_jsxs(Heading,{level:`2`,id:`langchain-v1-3-0`,children:[_jsx(_components.code,{children:`langchain`}),` v1.3.0`]}),_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[`This release adds support for `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`version=\"v3\"`}),` in `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`stream_events`}),` / `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`astream_events`}),` for `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`langchain`}),` agents. Refer to the `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/langchain/event-streaming`,children:`event streaming`}),` guide for details.`]})]}),`\n`,_jsxs(Update,{label:`May 12, 2026`,tags:[`langgraph`],rss:{title:`May 12, 2026 - langgraph`},id:`may-12%2C-2026-3`,children:[_jsxs(Heading,{level:`2`,id:`langgraph-v1-2-0`,children:[_jsx(_components.code,{children:`langgraph`}),` v1.2.0`]}),_jsx(_components.p,{children:`This release adds finer-grained control over node execution (timeouts, error recovery, and graceful shutdown), a new channel type that cuts checkpoint overhead for long-running threads, and a new content-block-centric streaming API (v3) with typed, per-channel projections.`}),_jsxs(_components.ul,{children:[`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[`\n`,_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[_jsxs(_components.strong,{children:[_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/langgraph/pregel#deltachannel`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`DeltaChannel`})}),` (beta)`]}),`: A new channel type that stores only the incremental delta at each step rather than re-serializing the full accumulated value. Most useful for channels that grow large over time, for example a message list in a long-running thread. Use `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`snapshot_frequency=K`}),` to write a full snapshot every K steps and bound read latency.`]}),`\n`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[`\n`,_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/langgraph/fault-tolerance#timeouts`,children:`Per-node timeouts`})}),`: Pass `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`timeout=`}),` to `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://reference.langchain.com/python/langgraph/graph/state/StateGraph/add_node`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`add_node`})}),` to cap how long a single attempt may run. See the `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/migrate/langchain-mcp-adapters`,children:`migration guide`}),`.`]})]}),`\n`,_jsxs(Update,{label:`Jul 24, 2026`,tags:[`deepagents`],rss:{title:`Jul 24, 2026 - deepagents`},id:`jul-24%2C-2026`,children:[_jsxs(Heading,{level:`2`,id:`deepagents-v0-7-0`,children:[_jsx(_components.code,{children:`deepagents`}),` v0.7.0`]}),_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[`A leaner, more configurable harness by default. On a default-agent turn, input tokens drop `,_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`65%`}),` (5,395 → 1,895), validated against our `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://www.langchain.com/blog/how-we-benchmark-deep-agents`,children:`revamped evaluation suite`}),` with no quality regression.`]}),_jsx(Heading,{level:`3`,id:`optimizations`,children:`Optimizations`}),_jsxs(_components.ul,{children:[`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`Lean prompts by default`}),`: The authored base prompt starts empty and tool-usage prose that duplicated tool schemas has been trimmed. Isolated to the default agent’s tool schemas, total description tokens drop `,_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`43%`}),` (4,005 → 2,302); combined with the empty base prompt and opt-in todos, a default-agent turn’s input tokens drop `,_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`65%`}),` (5,395 → 1,895). Tool behavior is unchanged. (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4859`,children:`#4859`}),`, `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4979`,children:`#4979`}),`, `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/5009`,children:`#5009`}),`)`]}),`\n`]}),_jsx(Heading,{level:`3`,id:`features-3`,children:`Features`}),_jsxs(_components.ul,{children:[`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/deepagents/customization#middleware`,children:`Override a default middleware instance`})}),`: A `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`middleware=`}),` (or subagent `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`middleware`}),`) instance whose `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`.name`}),` matches a built-in now replaces that default in place, rather than erroring on a duplicate. For example, pass your own `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`SummarizationMiddleware(...)`}),` to change the token trigger or summary model without disabling the built-in default. (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4251`,children:`#4251`}),`)`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`Filesystem tools`}),`: New `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/deepagents/tools#built-in-harness-tools`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`delete`})}),` tool removes a file or recursively removes a directory (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/3659`,children:`#3659`}),`, `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/3851`,children:`#3851`}),`); `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`write_file`}),` now overwrites an existing file instead of erroring (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4109`,children:`#4109`}),`); `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`FilesystemMiddleware`}),` accepts a `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/deepagents/overview#virtual-filesystem-access`,children:`tool allowlist`}),` to expose only selected built-in tools (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4325`,children:`#4325`}),`, `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4698`,children:`#4698`}),`); and reads and searches are tuned for open models — paginated `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`read_file`}),` reports total and remaining lines plus the next `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`offset`}),` (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4540`,children:`#4540`}),`), `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`grep`}),`/`,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`glob`}),` return partial results with a `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`truncated`}),` flag instead of hanging on large trees (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4063`,children:`#4063`}),`), and `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`grep`}),` gains a 1,000-match cap with streamed output and optional context lines (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4570`,children:`#4570`}),`, `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4706`,children:`#4706`}),`).`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`More prompt-caching support`}),`: Bedrock prompt caching via the `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`deepagents[aws]`}),` extra (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/issues/4108`,children:`#4108`}),`), and automatic Fireworks prompt-cache session affinity (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4598`,children:`#4598`}),`).`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`NVIDIA support`}),`: A built-in Nemotron 3 Ultra harness profile plus NIM app-origin attribution. (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4192`,children:`#4192`}),`, `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4455`,children:`#4455`}),`)`]}),`\n`]}),_jsx(Heading,{level:`3`,id:`breaking-changes-2`,children:`Breaking changes`}),_jsxs(_components.ul,{children:[`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`Planning todos are opt-in`}),`: `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`create_deep_agent`}),` no longer includes `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`TodoListMiddleware`}),` by default, so the `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`write_todos`}),` tool, `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`todos`}),` state channel, and todo-planning prompt are absent unless restored with `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`middleware=[TodoListMiddleware()]`}),`. (The OpenAI Codex harness profile still opts in automatically.) (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4929`,children:`#4929`}),`)`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`Backend compatibility shims removed`}),`: Pass concrete `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`BackendProtocol`}),` instances instead of factories, configure `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`StoreBackend`}),` with an explicit `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`namespace`}),`, and use the current `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`ls`}),` / `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`glob`}),` / `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`grep`}),` / `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`ReadResult`}),` APIs. Removed symbols include `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`BackendFactory`}),`, `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`BACKEND_TYPES`}),`, `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`FileFormat`}),`, and `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`Unset`}),`. New files store string `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`FileData.content`}),`; older `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`list[str]`}),` content stays readable and converts on next write. (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4541`,children:`#4541`}),`)`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`Output format changes`}),`: Empty `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`ls`}),` / `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`glob`}),` output is now `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`No files found`}),` instead of `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`[]`}),`, and `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`read_file`}),` no longer renders a fixed-width `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`cat -n`}),`-style gutter — update any parsers of raw tool output. (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4561`,children:`#4561`}),`)`]}),`\n`]}),_jsx(_components.p,{children:`Copy the following prompt into your AI coding assistant to migrate a codebase for these breaking changes:`}),_jsx(Prompt,{description:`Migrate a deepagents codebase from v0.6.x to v0.7.`,icon:`arrow-right`,actions:[`copy`],children:'Migrate this codebase from `deepagents` v0.6.x to v0.7 to account for the following breaking changes:\\n\\n1. `create_deep_agent` no longer includes `TodoListMiddleware` by default. If this codebase relies on the `write_todos` tool, the `todos` state channel, or the todo-planning prompt, restore it by importing `TodoListMiddleware` from `langchain.agents.middleware` (not `deepagents`) and passing it to `create_deep_agent`:\\n\\n   ```python  theme={\"theme\":{\"light\":\"catppuccin-latte\",\"dark\":\"catppuccin-mocha\"}}\\n   from langchain.agents.middleware import TodoListMiddleware\\n   from deepagents import create_deep_agent\\n\\n   agent = create_deep_agent(middleware=[TodoListMiddleware()])\\n   ```\\n\\n2. Backend compatibility shims were removed: `BackendFactory`, `BACKEND_TYPES`, `FileFormat`, and `Unset` no longer exist. Replace any backend factories with concrete `BackendProtocol` instances, and add an explicit `namespace` to every `StoreBackend` configuration:\\n\\n   ```python  theme={\"theme\":{\"light\":\"catppuccin-latte\",\"dark\":\"catppuccin-mocha\"}}\\n   from deepagents import create_deep_agent\\n   from deepagents.backends import StoreBackend\\n\\n   # Before (v0.6.x): factory callable, and StoreBackend with no explicit namespace\\n   agent = create_deep_agent(backend=lambda rt: StoreBackend())  # [!code --]\\n\\n   # After (v0.7): concrete backend instance with an explicit namespace\\n   agent = create_deep_agent(backend=StoreBackend(namespace=lambda rt: (rt.server_info.user.identity,)))  # [!code ++]\\n   ```\\n\\n   Also update calls to use the current `ls`, `glob`, `grep`, and `ReadResult` APIs.\\n\\n3. Tool output formats changed: empty `ls` / `glob` output is now the string `No files found` instead of `[]`, and `read_file` no longer renders a fixed-width `cat -n`-style line-number gutter. Update any code that parses these tool outputs.\\n\\nSearch the codebase for usages of the removed symbols and for parsing logic that depends on the old output formats, apply the necessary changes, and flag anything that needs manual review.\\n'})]}),`\n`,_jsxs(Update,{label:`May 12, 2026`,tags:[`deepagents`],rss:{title:`May 12, 2026 - langchain`},id:`may-12%2C-2026`,children:[_jsxs(Heading,{level:`2`,id:`deepagents-v0-6-0`,children:[_jsx(_components.code,{children:`deepagents`}),` v0.6.0`]}),_jsxs(_components.ul,{children:[`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/deepagents/interpreters`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`CodeInterpreterMiddleware`})})}),`: (experimental) `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`deepagents`}),` now supports code execution and programmatic tool calling through a scoped QuickJS runtime.`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[`Supports `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`version=\"v3\"`}),` in `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`stream_events`}),` / `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`astream_events`}),`. Refer to the `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/deepagents/event-streaming`,children:`event streaming`}),` guide for details.`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsxs(_components.strong,{children:[_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/langgraph/pregel#deltachannel`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`DeltaChannel`})}),` (beta)`]}),` (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://www.langchain.com/blog/delta-channels-evolving-agent-runtime`,children:`blog`}),`): Deep Agents now uses `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`DeltaChannel`}),` for message history and agent files. Rather than re-serializing the full accumulated value into every checkpoint, only the incremental delta written at each step is stored — keeping checkpoint sizes small as threads grow long.`]}),`\n`]}),_jsx(Warning,{children:_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`Rolling back from v0.6.0 is not supported once threads have persisted.`}),` Deep Agents v0.6.0 changes persisted message history and agent files to `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`DeltaChannel`}),`, which writes checkpoints in a new format that earlier versions cannot read. Downgrading to an earlier Deep Agents version switches these channels back to non-delta channels, leaving existing delta checkpoints unreadable and causing incomplete or incorrect state reconstruction. If you need to roll back, use the `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/langchain-ai/langgraph/tree/main/examples/delta-channel-dump`,children:`delta-channel-dump recovery script`}),` to migrate affected threads, or discard them, before downgrading. More generally, avoid switching a persisted channel between delta and non-delta representations. See `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/langgraph/pregel#version-compatibility-and-rollbacks`,children:`Version compatibility and channel changes`}),`.`]})}),_jsxs(_components.ul,{children:[`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/deepagents/profiles`,children:`Harness profiles`})}),`: Register per-provider or per-model configuration bundles (`,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`HarnessProfile`}),`) that `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`create_deep_agent`}),` applies automatically when a model is selected — system-prompt tweaks, tool overrides, middleware changes, and subagent defaults — without modifying the call site.`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/deepagents/backends#contexthubbackend`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`ContextHubBackend`})})}),` (`,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://www.langchain.com/blog/introducing-context-hub`,children:`blog`}),`): A new filesystem backend backed by LangSmith Hub. Agent files — skills, memories, and other persisted context — are stored as Hub commits, giving you version history on every write and LangSmith-native durability without provisioning a separate LangGraph store.`]}),`\n`]})]}),`\n`,_jsxs(Update,{label:`May 12, 2026`,tags:[`langchain`],rss:{title:`May 12, 2026 - langchain`},id:`may-12%2C-2026-2`,children:[_jsxs(Heading,{level:`2`,id:`langchain-v1-3-0`,children:[_jsx(_components.code,{children:`langchain`}),` v1.3.0`]}),_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[`This release adds support for `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`version=\"v3\"`}),` in `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`stream_events`}),` / `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`astream_events`}),` for `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`langchain`}),` agents. Most useful for channels that grow large over time, for example a message list in a long-running thread. Use `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`snapshot_frequency=K`}),` to write a full snapshot every K steps and bound read latency.`]}),`\n`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[`\n`,_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/langgraph/fault-tolerance#timeouts`,children:`Per-node timeouts`})}),`: Pass `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`timeout=`}),` to `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://reference.langchain.com/python/langgraph/graph/state/StateGraph/add_node`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`add_node`})}),` to cap how long a single attempt may run. Set a hard wall-clock limit (`,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`run_timeout`}),`), an idle limit that resets on progress (`,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`idle_timeout`}),`), or both via `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://reference.langchain.com/python/langgraph/types/TimeoutPolicy`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`TimeoutPolicy`})}),`. When the limit fires, LangGraph raises `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://reference.langchain.com/python/langgraph/errors/NodeTimeoutError`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`NodeTimeoutError`})}),`, clears writes from that attempt, and hands off to the retry policy. Async nodes only.`]}),`\n`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[`\n`,_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/langgraph/fault-tolerance#error-handling`,children:`Node-level error handlers`})}),`: Pass `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`error_handler=`}),` to `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://reference.langchain.com/python/langgraph/graph/state/StateGraph/add_node`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`add_node`})}),` to run a recovery function after all retries are exhausted. The handler receives a typed `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://reference.langchain.com/python/langgraph/errors/NodeError`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`NodeError`})}),` and can return a `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://reference.langchain.com/python/langgraph/types/Command`,children:_jsx(_components.code,{children:`Command`})}),` to update state and route to a different node, useful for Saga/compensation patterns.`]}),`\n`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[`\n`,_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:_jsx(_components.a,{href:`/oss/python/langgraph/fault-tolerance#graceful-shutdown`,children:`Graceful shutdown`})}),`: Stop an in-flight run cooperatively after the current superstep completes, and save a resumable checkpoint. the run raises `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`GraphDrained`}),` and can be resumed later with the same config.`]}),`\n`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[`\n`,_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`New event streaming API (beta)`}),`: Pass `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`version=\"v3\"`}),` to `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`stream_events()`}),` / `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`astream_events()`}),` for a content-block-centric protocol with typed, per-channel projections (`,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`run.values`}),`, `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`run.messages`}),`, `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`run.lifecycle`}),`, `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`run.subgraphs`}),`) plus opt-in transformers for updates, custom events, checkpoints, tasks, and debug. `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`run.messages`}),` yields one `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`ChatModelStream`}),` per LLM call with typed sub-projections for text, reasoning, tool calls, and usage. `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`version=\"v1\"`}),` and `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`version=\"v2\"`}),` are unchanged.`]}),`\n`]}),`\n`]}),_jsx(_components.p,{children:`Timeouts and error handlers are Python-only;
